+### AD-026 — Minimal MVP V1 historical Backtest-to-WFA qualification and stage authority
+
+**Status:** PROSPECTIVE M9.5 DESIGN/ADMISSION SUCCESSOR — requires exact publication read-back, independent Review 2 PASS and explicit final owner acceptance for completed admission. **Target:** M9.5. This section does not authorize code or behavioral-specification publication.
+
+This decision is a bounded prospective successor to the **qualification-criteria and qualification-robustness portion only** of accepted AD-021. The exact accepted AD-021 paragraph currently reads: "Complete trial accounting, declared search, holdout/OOS discipline, benchmarks, parameter sensitivity, cost/slippage stress, temporal robustness, cross-sectional breadth, WFA and explicit insufficiency are V1 research requirements." In MVP V1, **complete trial accounting**, **declared search**, **holdout/OOS discipline**, **descriptive cross-sectional breadth**, **WFA/OOS stage integrity**, and **explicit insufficiency** remain mandatory research requirements. Benchmark performance/outperformance qualification, parameter sensitivity testing, **additional** cost/slippage stress scenarios beyond the already accepted execution-cost rules, and **additional** temporal/regime robustness qualification are deferred to a later separately approved version as economic **qualification tests**; they are not required criteria for the MVP historical Backtest-to-WFA eligibility gate. **Cross-sectional breadth remains a mandatory descriptive research-reporting and lineage requirement; it is NOT being deferred.** Any future cross-instrument performance robustness threshold is a distinct optional qualification criterion, **not a replacement name for accepted cross-sectional breadth**. The already mandatory realistic transaction-cost accounting, genuine data/evidence checks, immutable complete Trial/search history and applicable release safeguards remain mandatory. This prospective change does not assert that the currently published AD-021 wording was edited or deleted; AD-021 is preserved verbatim in its accepted historical location. The precise original-versus-prospective-effective wording is shown in this CCRF's normal human comparison. All other AD-021 contracts, including immutable registered research identity and complete Trial lineage, authoritative evidence validation, separate validity/qualification dimensions, append-only decisions, immutable Candidate revisions, true WFA/OOS-before-qualified-Paper progression, truthful independent-account aggregation, no real-money V1 trading and AI non-authority, remain operative. No accepted M3–M8 financial, data-source, WFA or causal Paper implementation is reopened.
+
+For **MVP V1 M9.5 historical Backtest-to-WFA admission**, the **sole economic qualification criterion** is `net_profitable_trade_rate_pct >= T` (inclusive), where **`T` is a human-selected fixed percentage recorded in the applicable immutable, versioned `QualificationPolicy`**. `T` must be a finite percentage within 0 through 100, inclusive; no universal numeric threshold is hard-coded into this architecture decision, human-language specification family, or evaluation formula. The authorized human selects `T` before the policy is used for evaluation; a policy version fixes one exact `T` for every evaluation under that version. A later human decision to use a different `T` creates a **new immutable policy version** and new append-only evaluation decisions without editing previously approved general design documentation, modifying historical decisions/evidence or silently changing the threshold for an existing policy version. Creation/activation of a new policy version remains subject to the separately approved behavior, permissions and audit controls established by later implementation-ready specifications; this design grants no unconstrained runtime edit authority. Reuse `PerformanceMetrics.summarize_backtest` semantics: numerator = completed trades whose monetary net `trade.pnl` is strictly greater than zero after effective costs; denominator = all completed trades, including losses and breakevens. Compare the unrounded computed value to the exact unrounded stored policy threshold, never display-rounded values or an untrusted client-provided rate. **This is a winning-trade percentage, not positive total net profit or account return.** One profitable trade out of one completed trade may meet the sole criterion under a permissive human-selected threshold; this is an explicitly disclosed MVP limitation, not an implied statistical sufficiency claim.
+
+The minimum evaluation subject is **one expressly selected, verified, registered historical Backtest/Trial result** and the exact linked fixed strategy/procedure, effective configuration, and immutable evidence identity. Preserve the associated StudyRevision and complete registered Trial/search denominator as historical context; do not convert independent Trial-account aggregation into the selected result's trade rate, automatically pick the best result, or relabel prior observed development data as untouched OOS. Missing required trustworthy evidence or zero completed trades is non-eligible `INSUFFICIENT`; evidence failing integrity or identity validation is `INVALID` **on the validity axis**, not an additional economic criterion. With valid sufficient evidence, meeting the economic criterion produces qualification `ELIGIBLE` and failing it produces `REJECTED`. The later human-language specification must map data validity, per-criterion outcomes, overall qualification decisions, and operational evaluation errors separately, consistent with AD-021.
+
+A versioned immutable `QualificationPolicy` fixes the selected threshold `T` for **that policy version**, not for all future versions; the economic criterion remains exactly one. A deterministic evaluator must preserve exact inputs, the exact applied threshold, and observed counts/rate. Each evaluation creates an attributable append-only `QualificationDecision` bound to the policy version, evaluator version, selected evidence, exact fixed `CandidateRevision` and reasoned result; policy changes or reevaluation never rewrite old evidence or decisions. Initial MVP implementation may support fixed candidates only; accepted AD-021 adaptive-definition semantics are preserved for a separately approved later extension, not silently weakened or misapplied to fixed claims.
+
+`ELIGIBLE` is only a permission to hand **the same exact CandidateRevision** to M9.6 for independent Candidate-bound WFA/OOS confirmation. It neither launches WFA automatically nor certifies WFA or profitability, authorizes qualified Paper, or enables live orders. M9.6 remains responsible for WFA/OOS assessment. M9.7 qualified Paper still requires its own applicable eligible WFA/OOS-stage decision for the unchanged CandidateRevision; existing operational Paper smoke is separately labelled.
+
+**Deferred as additional M9.5/V1 economic qualification gates**, not as research-integrity safeguards: independent minimum-trade-count/sample-size thresholds, benchmark outperformance, parameter stability/sensitivity tests, additional cost/slippage stress scenarios, statistical confidence/multiple-testing methods, temporal/regime return thresholds, cross-instrument robustness thresholds, Sharpe, Sortino, profit factor, drawdown thresholds, configurable rule builders and adaptive-candidate qualification. This deferral is to a **later separately approved version**, without declaring any such future version active. It does not remove actual Backtest transaction costs, authoritative drawdown accounting, historical trial lineage, valid WFA/OOS partitions, existing release-stage safety obligations or the prohibition on fabricated evidence. The central Deferred Work ledger requires separately controlled indexing/synchronization before M9.5 code implementation; this design records the complete deferral decisions immediately and does not falsely claim the ledger has already been modified.
+
+**Admission rule:** M9.5 is admitted **for incremental natural-language specification preparation only** when this exact bounded design publication passes authoritative remote read-back, independent actual-state Review 2 PASS, and explicit final Human Approval 3. Before all three gates, M9.5 remains `NOT_ADMITTED`. Even after admission, **implementation remains `NOT_AUTHORIZED`** until the separately prepared exact human-language behavior specification and exact controlled code/file changes complete the applicable SOP Review 1 and human authorization. The withdrawn earlier proposed StudyRevision-protection SPEC001 is not reinstated, and no permanent new implementation specification ID is reserved by this design decision.

Appendix E — exact V002 → V003 draft changes (precise changed portions only)

This separately compares the two unpublished design drafts; it is not a Git source diff. Changed words are highlighted without styling unchanged words.

docs/architecture/DECISIONS.md — AD-026 economic threshold and versioning

For **MVP V1 M9.5 historical Backtest-to-WFA admission**, the **sole economic qualification criterion** is the authoritative `net_profitable_trade_rate_pct >= 50.0T` (inclusive), where **`T` is a human-selected fixed percentage recorded in the applicable immutable, versioned `QualificationPolicy`**. `T` must be a finite percentage within 0 through 100, inclusive; no universal numeric threshold is hard-coded into this architecture decision, human-language specification family, or evaluation formula. The authorized human selects `T` before the policy is used for evaluation; a policy version fixes one exact `T` for every evaluation under that version. A later human decision to use a different `T` creates a **new immutable policy version** and new append-only evaluation decisions without editing previously approved general design documentation, modifying historical decisions/evidence or silently changing the threshold for an existing policy version. Creation/activation of a new policy version remains subject to the separately approved behavior, permissions and audit controls established by later implementation-ready specifications; this design grants no unconstrained runtime edit authority. Reuse `PerformanceMetrics.summarize_backtest` semantics: numerator = completed trades whose monetary net `trade.pnl` is strictly greater than zero after effective costs; denominator = all completed trades, including losses and breakevens. Compare the unrounded computed value to the exact unrounded stored policy threshold, never a display-rounded values or an untrusted client-provided rate. **This is a winning-trade percentage, not positive total net profit or account return.** One profitable trade out of one completed trade may meet the sole criterion under a permissive human-selected threshold; this is an explicitly disclosed MVP limitation, not an implied statistical sufficiency claim.

docs/architecture/DECISIONS.md — AD-026 immutable policy

A versioned immutable `QualificationPolicy` fixes the selected threshold `T` for **that policy vers ion**, not for all future versions; the economic criterion fremains exactly or the applicable M9.5 stagne. A deterministic evaluator must preserve exact inputs, the exact applied threshold, and observed counts/rate. Each evaluation creates an attributable append-only `QualificationDecision` bound to the policy version, evaluator version, selected evidence, exact fixed `CandidateRevision` and reasoned result; policy changes or reevaluation never rewrite old evidence or decisions. Initial MVP implementation may support fixed candidates only; accepted AD-021 adaptive-definition semantics are preserved for a separately approved later extension, not silently weakened or misapplied to fixed claims.

docs/roadmap/ROADMAP.md — Current M9.5 roadmap entry

  - **M9.5 - ADMISSION/DESIGN PUBLICATION PROPOSED; IMPLEMENTATION NOT AUTHORIZED** - Under prospective AD-026, the sole MVP V1 Backtest-to-WFA economic criterion is `net_profitable_trade_rate_pct >= 50.0T` for the human-selected fixed percentage `T` stored in the applicable immutable versioned policy, applied to one explicit valid registered historical Trial result. Changing `T` requires a successor policy version and append-only new decisions, not an edit to the generic design documentation. Formal admission for small human-language specifications occurs only after exact admission-design publication/read-back, independent Review 2 PASS, and explicit final owner acceptance; until those gates M9.5 remains NOT_ADMITTED. No M9.5 code, WFA or Paper implementation is authorized by this design publication; later behavioral-specification and code-change approvals remain mandatory. Broader robustness qualification gates are proposed for later approved versions, while data/evidence integrity and WFA-before-qualified-Paper invariants remain required.

docs/validation/VALIDATION_PLAN.md — M9.5 selected percentage evaluation

Subject to separately approved human-language specifications, the minimum M9.5 behavior will evaluate one explicitly selected and verified registered historical Backtest/Trial result for one fixed CandidateRevision using **only** the unrounded `net_profitable_trade_rate_pct >= 50.0T` economic criterion, where `T` is the human-selected fixed percentage in the applicable immutable, versioned policy (finite, within 0–100 inclusive). The human selects a specific threshold before evaluation; no universal number is embedded in the general design. Positive monetary **net** completed-trade P&L counts as profitable; losing and breakeven completed trades remain in the denominator. Evidence validity, identity and availability are prerequisites to legitimate evaluation, not extra economic criteria. Zero completed trades is insufficient rather than rejected. Failure to meet the 50% criterion wWith valid sufficient evidence, a calculated rate below the selected policy threshold `T` is rejected;, mand a rate **equal to or above that poling exactly 50.0%threshold** is eligible **for M9.6 consideration only**.

docs/validation/VALIDATION_PLAN.md — M9.5 threshold boundary tests

Subsequent deterministic tests must cover exact 50% equality to **each selected policy threshold** `T`, just-below threshold, finite percentage bounds (including 0 and 100), invalid/missing policy threshold, immutable per-policy-version threshold identity, explicit human selection and successor-policy decisions when the human chooses a different threshold, breakevens, zero completed trades, invalid/absent evidence, net-versus-gross-cost classification, unchanged precision/authoritative M4 economics, exact selected Trial and fixed CandidateRevision bindings, append-only policy/decision identities, no hidden best-Trial selection, no WFA/Paper auto-launch, and unchanged accepted safety and stage-order regressions. OEven one winning completed trade cmany meet a sufficiently permissive human-selected percentage threshold; this is not a claim of adeliberquate statisticaly low-sample economsic rulze. Eligibility does not claim aggregate profitability, validated WFA/OOS results or permission for qualified Paper.
